# SwiftUI: what Isomer does not have yet

Every declaration of Apple's SwiftUI interface (iOS 27.0 SDK) that Isomer's SwiftUI lacks, with Apple's signature: the list to choose what to implement next from. [SwiftUI.md](../SwiftUI.md) has the counts, the reasons and what is implemented differently. Generated by `tools/parity/parity.py` on 2026-10-19 with Isomer at `8fa42f41`.

Areas are ordered planned, then maybe, then the rest, larger first; within an area, types with the most missing first. "The type too" marks a type Isomer does not declare at all; elsewhere the type is there and these members are not. A version is the iOS release that introduced the declaration.

| Area | Missing | Intent |
|---|---|---|
| [Shapes, colors and geometry](#shapes-colors-and-geometry-568) | 568 | planned |
| [Styles](#styles-497) | 497 | planned |
| [Animation and transitions](#animation-and-transitions-465) | 465 | planned |
| [Shaders, drawing and rendering](#shaders-drawing-and-rendering-317) | 317 | planned (shader effects with Isomer's Metal, after 0.31) |
| [Controls](#controls-317) | 317 | planned |
| [Layout](#layout-300) | 300 | planned |
| [Text and fonts](#text-and-fonts-280) | 280 | planned |
| [Navigation, tabs and search](#navigation-tabs-and-search-275) | 275 | planned |
| [Documents, scenes, windows and commands](#documents-scenes-windows-and-commands-183) | 183 | planned |
| [View modifiers](#view-modifiers-181) | 181 | planned |
| [Drag and drop, sharing and the pasteboard](#drag-and-drop-sharing-and-the-pasteboard-156) | 156 | planned |
| [Scrolling](#scrolling-129) | 129 | planned |
| [Gestures](#gestures-93) | 93 | planned |
| [Data flow](#data-flow-73) | 73 | planned |
| [Environment values](#environment-values-59) | 59 | planned |
| [Other declarations](#other-declarations-539) | 539 | planned, as apps need them |
| [New in iOS 27](#new-in-ios-27-282) | 282 | planned, as the rest of the interface is |
| [Accessibility](#accessibility-344) | 344 | maybe (each renderer's accessibility API) |
| [Tables](#tables-250) | 250 | maybe |
| [Haptic feedback](#haptic-feedback-55) | 55 | maybe |
| Total | 5363 | |

After the areas, what Isomer's types lack beyond declarations: conformances 112, Sendable 22, overloads with other parameter types 279.

Most missing by type (all areas):

`View` 529, `GraphicsContext` 157*, `Text` 143, `TableColumn` 94*, `EnvironmentValues` 84, `ModifiedContent` 78, `Font` 64, `SystemFormatStyle` 60*, `Color` 58, `Edge` 51, `SensoryFeedback` 51*, `AttributedTextFormatting` 47*, `Image` 42, `ShapeStyle` 40, `TabContent` 40, `Tab` 40, `Shape` 38, `Picker` 37, `Table` 35*, `MeshGradient` 32*, `Spring` 31*, `Shader` 31*, `WindowGroup` 30, `DropSession` 29*, `Scene` 27, `BlendMode` 25*, `Animation` 25, `Path` 23, `ColorMatrix` 23*, `RoundedRectangularShapeCorners` 22*

(* the type itself is missing.)

## Shapes, colors and geometry (568)

Why: The rest of shapes and their transforms, colors and gradients, blend modes, angles and insets.

Intent: planned.

## Styles (497)

Why: The style protocols and their configurations (`ButtonStyle`, `ToggleStyle`, `LabelStyle`, `GaugeStyle`…); Isomer's controls take fixed style options.

Intent: planned.

## Animation and transitions (465)

Why: Animation curves and springs, `Animatable`, transitions, keyframe and phase animators, matched geometry.

Intent: planned.

## Shaders, drawing and rendering (317)

Why: Shaders and the visual effects that take them, `GraphicsContext`, `Canvas`, mesh gradients, color matrices, `ImageRenderer`.

Intent: planned (shader effects with Isomer's Metal, after 0.31).

## Text and fonts (280)

Why: Text formatting and attributed text, text selection and input formatting, the rest of `Font` and `Text`'s modifiers.

Intent: planned.

Intent: planned.

## Haptic feedback (55)

Intent: maybe.

## Conformances Isomer's types lack (112)

Types both have, conforming on Apple to protocols Isomer's do not (Sendable is below): code that compares, hashes, encodes, prints or uses one so builds with Apple's only.

- `Angle`: `Animatable`, `Comparable`, `Decodable`, `Encodable`, `Hashable`
- `Animation`: `CustomDebugStringConvertible`, `CustomReflectable`, `CustomStringConvertible`, `Hashable`
- `AttributeScopes.SwiftUIAttributes.BackgroundColorAttribute`: `DecodableAttributedStringKey`, `EncodableAttributedStringKey`
- `AttributeScopes.SwiftUIAttributes.FontAttribute`: `DecodableAttributedStringKey`, `EncodableAttributedStringKey`
- `AttributeScopes.SwiftUIAttributes.ForegroundColorAttribute`: `DecodableAttributedStringKey`, `EncodableAttributedStringKey`
- `AttributeScopes.SwiftUIAttributes.StrikethroughStyleAttribute`: `DecodableAttributedStringKey`, `EncodableAttributedStringKey`
- `AttributeScopes.SwiftUIAttributes.UnderlineStyleAttribute`: `DecodableAttributedStringKey`, `EncodableAttributedStringKey`
- `Axis`: `CustomStringConvertible`
- `ButtonBorderShape`: `Animatable`, `InsettableShape`, `Shape`, `View`
- `Capsule`: `Animatable`, `RoundedRectangularShape`
- `Circle`: `Animatable`, `RoundedRectangularShape`
- `Color`: `CustomStringConvertible`, `Transferable`
- `ColorScheme`: `CaseIterable`
- `ContentMode`: `CaseIterable`
- `ControlSize`: `Comparable`
- `DefaultGlassEffectShape`: `Animatable`
- `EdgeInsets`: `Animatable`
- `Ellipse`: `Animatable`
- `EmptyView`: `Commands`, `CustomizableToolbarContent`, `SceneAccessoryContent`, `ToolbarContent`
- `Font.TextStyle`: `Decodable`, `Encodable`
- `ForEach`: `AccessibilityRotorContent`, `CustomizableToolbarContent`, `DynamicTableRowContent`, `SceneAccessoryContent`, `TabContent`, `TableRowContent`, `ToolbarContent`
- `Glass`: `Equatable`
- `Gradient`: `Hashable`, `ShapeStyle`
- `Gradient.Stop`: `Hashable`
- `GridItem`: `Equatable`
- `GridItem.Size`: `Equatable`
- `Group`: `AccessibilityRotorContent`, `Commands`, `CustomizableToolbarContent`, `Scene`, `SceneAccessoryContent`, `TabContent`, `TableColumnContent`, `TableRowContent`, `ToolbarContent`
- `HorizontalEdge`: `CaseIterable`, `Decodable`, `Encodable`, `RawRepresentable`
- `Image`: `Equatable`, `Transferable`
- `Image.Orientation`: `CaseIterable`
- `KeyPress`: `CustomDebugStringConvertible`
- `KeyPress.Phases`: `CustomDebugStringConvertible`
- `ModifiedContent`: `Animatable`, `CustomHoverEffect`, `DynamicTableRowContent`, `DynamicViewContent`, `Equatable`, `Scene`, `TableRowContent`, `ViewModifier`, `VisualEffect`, `s:7SwiftUI18HoverEffectContentP`

## Sendable on Apple, not in Isomer (22)

Code that passes one of these across actors, or keeps one in a Sendable type, builds with Apple's only (Swift 6 language mode).

`AngularGradient`, `AnyShapeStyle`, `BackgroundStyle`, `Capsule`, `Circle`, `DefaultGlassEffectShape`, `DragGesture.Value`, `Ellipse`, `EmptyView`, `GlassEffectContainer`, `HierarchicalShapeStyle`, `Image`, `LinearGradient`, `MagnifyGesture.Value`, `ModifiedContent`, `RadialGradient`, `Rectangle`, `RoundedRectangle`, `SimultaneousGesture.Value`, `Spacer`, `Text`, `TintShapeStyle`
